Method: 提出AISAC闭环框架，定义三向张力（感知-通信-学习），论证经典最优感知配置未必有利于学习，主张物理层配置应以学习对齐为设计原则。

Result: 揭示了AISAC的核心矛盾：感知精度与学习效用不一致，提出需重新设计物理层；给出了车载边缘智能用例及理论、实现、标准化方面的开放问题。

Conclusion: AISAC要求新的物理层设计原则，即配置ISAC波形/波束/功率/带宽/感知模式时应以学习对齐为目标，而非单纯追求感知失真或通信速率。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) and AI-and-communication (AIAC) are identified as separate usage scenarios in the ITU IMT-2030 vision for sixth-generation (6G) networks. In practice, however, these two directions are already beginning to merge. ISAC gives the network a way to observe the physical world, while AI gives the network a way to learn from those observations and act on them. This article introduces AI-integrated sensing and communication (AISAC) as a closed-loop framework for this merger. In AISAC, AI is not only a tool used to optimize an ISAC system. ISAC is also the physical substrate through which AI receives data, context, and connectivity. The key technical message is that AISAC requires a new physical-layer design principle, in which the ISAC waveform, beam, power, bandwidth, and sensing mode should be configured for learning alignment, not for sensing distortion or communication rate alone. In particular, the sensing configuration that is most accurate from a classical estimation viewpoint need not be the one that is most useful for training or inference. We present the AISAC landscape, explain why imperfect sensing changes the learning problem, develop the closed-loop architecture and its three-way sensing-communication-learning tension, and outline a vehicular edge-intelligence use case together with open problems for theory, implementation, and standardization.

</details>


### [249] [Variable Rate Lossy Source-Channel Coding over Channels with Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16600)
*Timothy Liu,Fady Alajaji,Tamás Linder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于突发噪声通信信道的变速率有损联合信源信道编码方案，通过带反馈的多级信道优化矢量量化和贪心比特分配算法，在M阶马尔可夫噪声Polya传染信道上相比固定速率方案实现了高达约4.5dB的SNR增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对突发噪声通信信道，现有固定速率联合信源信道编码方案在信道条件变化时灵活性不足，需要一种能根据反馈信息动态调整比特分配的变速率方案来提升性能。

Method: 提出变速率有损联合信源信道编码方案，采用多级信道优化矢量量化系统，通过无噪反馈链路获取信道输出序列，利用贪心算法在各阶段变速率残差量化器之间动态分配比特，推广了先前的固定速率方案。

Result: 在M阶马尔可夫噪声Polya传染信道上的仿真表明，所提变速率方案在不同信道误码率和噪声相关性下均优于固定速率方案，信号噪声比增益最高可达约4.5dB。

Conclusion: 变速率方案通过反馈自适应比特分配，在突发噪声信道上持续优于固定速率方案，验证了动态资源分配在恶劣信道条件下的有效性。

Abstract: A variable-rate lossy joint source-channel coding scheme for burst-noise communication channels with noiseless feedback is introduced. The scheme comprises a multi-stage channel optimized vector quantization system that dynamically allocates bits via a greedy algorithm among the variable-rate residual quantizers at each stage, based on the channel output sequence received at the encoder through the feedback link, thereby generalizing a prior fixed-rate scheme. Simulations over an $M$-th order Markov noise Polya contagion channel demonstrate that the proposed variable-rate scheme consistently outperforms the fixed-rate scheme, regardless of the channel bit error rate and noise correlation, achieving signal-to-noise ratio gains of up to about 4.5 dB.

</details>


### [250] [Leveraging von Mises Message-Passing for Massive MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16686)
*Sweta Suresh,Arthur Michon,Charly Poulliat,Maxime Guillaud,Claire Goursaud*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于方向统计学的置信传播检测器，用于PSK调制的mMIMO系统，通过连续松弛和von Mises参数化实现与调制阶数无关的低复杂度近似检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mMIMO系统中最优解码器（如MAP）的计算复杂度呈指数增长，需要低复杂度的近似检测方法。

Method: 基于置信传播，将PSK调制连续松弛到单位圆上，采用von Mises分布参数化表示消息，从而获得稀疏表示以避免无穷维消息处理。

Result: 所提方法实现了与PSK调制阶数无关的低复杂度检测，并扩展到了非完美信道场景。

Conclusion: 该方法在性能和复杂度之间取得了良好平衡，优于基于高斯近似的检测算法。

Abstract: Detection in massive multiple input multiple output (mMIMO) systems suffers from exponential complexity while using optimal decoders like maximum a posteriori (MAP). We propose a belief propagation-based detector based on directional statistics, applicable to mMIMO systems relying on PSK modulations. Thanks to a continuous relaxation of the PSK modulation to the unit circle, and to the use of von Mises parametric representations of the messages to obtain sparse representations of the (generally infinite dimensional) messages, the proposed method allows for approximate detection with a low complexity which does not depend on the PSK modulation order. Extensions of the algorithm to imperfect channel realizations are also present. We quantify the performance and complexity of the proposed approach and compare it with detection algorithms based on Gaussian approximation.

</details>


### [251] [Revisiting the Stability of the Ingleton Inequality: A Tropicalization-Free Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16773)
*Laszlo Csirmaz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无热带化的方法，重新审视了Matveev和Romashchenko (2026)关于Ingleton不等式稳定性的结果，简化了证明并改进了误差估计，同时通过发现新的熵不等式族解决了先前工作中的一个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Ingleton不等式在精确条件独立约束下对熵点成立，但先前研究利用复杂的热带概率空间框架来量化当条件互信息项小非零时不等式被违反的程度；本文旨在用更简化的方法重新审视这些稳定性结果。

Method: 采用完全无热带化的替代框架，通过简化概念和证明过程，推导显式误差项，并改进估计值。

Result: 实现了证明的显著简化，获得了更精确的误差项，改进了部分估计值，并发现了一个新的熵不等式无限族，解决了公开问题。

Conclusion: 无热带化方法能够有效简化稳定性分析，并为Ingleton不等式相关研究提供更直接的证明途径。

Abstract: The classical Ingleton inequality is known to hold for entropic points under specific exact conditional independence constraints. Recently, Matveev and Romashchenko (2026) investigated the stability of these implications, quantifying the extent to which the Ingleton inequality can be violated when a group of conditional mutual information terms is small but non-zero. While their proofs relied fundamentally on the complex framework of tropical probability spaces, we revisit these stability results using a completely tropicalization-free approach. By developing an alternative framework, we significantly streamline the underlying concepts and proofs, derive explicit error terms, and improve some estimates. Furthermore, we resolve an open problem posed in prior work by exhibiting a new infinite family of entropy inequalities that establishes the stability of the sum of two Ingleton expressions.

</details>


### [252] [Sustainable Air-Ground Integrated Coverage Networks: ISCC Architecture, Technologies, and Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16812)
*J. Liu,X. Zhang,M. Sheng,R. Zhang,N. Zhao,J. Wang,J. Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向6G和低空经济的空地一体化覆盖网络（AGICN）的可持续性问题，提出了一种集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）驱动的架构，并介绍了多维可持续性度量体系。实验表明，该方案在保持90%以上覆盖概率的同时，功耗降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AGICN研究主要关注覆盖增强，忽视了可持续性。随着6G和低空经济的发展，迫切需要平衡覆盖能力与碳效率，解决异构空地段间多维资源耦合带来的新挑战。

Method: 提出ISCC驱动的架构，通过闭环控制实现动态资源编排；引入覆盖运营效率、任务导向性能和全生命周期碳排放的多维可持续性度量体系；整合AI、混合预编码、集成感知与通信、无线信息与功率同传等使能技术。

Result: 在真实测试平台上实现了功耗降低20%，同时覆盖概率超过90%。

Conclusion: 可持续的AGICN对于未来绿色网络是可行的，ISCC架构能有效平衡覆盖能力与碳效率。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of sixth-generation (6G) networks and the low-altitude economy has accelerated the evolution of wireless infrastructures toward air-ground integrated coverage networks (AGICNs), which seamlessly fuse terrestrial and aerial communication resources. However, existing AGICN studies primarily focus on coverage enhancement, while ignoring sustainability. Pursuing sustainable AGICNs introduces new challenges due to the multidimensional resource coupling across heterogeneous air-ground segments. In view of this, this paper presents a comprehensive survey and tutorial on sustainable AGICNs, aiming to balance coverage capacity with carbon efficiency in low-altitude economies. An integrated sensing, communication, and computation (ISCC)-driven architecture, which enables dynamic resource orchestration through closed-loop control, is proposed. We thus introduce a multi-dimensional sustainability metric system, which covers operational efficiency, task-oriented performance, and full lifecycle carbon emissions, to quantify energy and carbon footprints. We review enabling technologies, including artificial intelligence, hybrid precoding, integrated sensing and communication, and simultaneous wireless information and power transfer, and discuss their integration into the ISCC framework to minimize energy consumption while maintaining robust coverage. Experimental results on a real-world testbed demonstrate a 20% reduction in power consumption while achieving over 90% coverage probability, highlighting the feasibility of sustainable AGICNs for future green networks.

</details>


### [253] [Decoding Desarguesian spread codes beyond half minimum distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16890)
*Ermes Franch,Chunlei Li,Angelica Piccirillo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Desarguesian spread码的最近邻译码能力，发现其可在最小距离一半之外实现唯一译码，并提出了一种能处理插入和删除错误的新译码算法，在删除维度不超过k-2时，即使错误总维度超过最小距离一半也能以低失败概率成功译码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spread码是一类著名的常维子空间度量码，具有最小距离2k和丰富的几何结构。本文旨在探索Desarguesian spread码的译码能力，特别是在超越最小距离一半的范围内实现唯一译码的可能性，并开发能够同时处理插入和删除错误的新译码算法。

Method: 本文首先分析了Desarguesian spread码的最近邻译码器特性，证明了其可在超越最小距离一半时仍保持唯一译码能力。基于此发现，提出了一种新型译码算法，该算法能同时处理增加或减少码字维度的插入和删除错误。算法设计中允许删除维度最多为k-2，即使插入和删除的总维度超过最小距离的一半，算法仍能以较小的失败概率成功译码。此外，还提出了两种改进方案，经验上可处理与最近邻译码器几乎相同数量的插入错误。

Result: 证明了Desarguesian spread码的最近邻译码器可在超越最小距离一半时实现唯一译码；提出的新译码算法在删除维度≤k-2时，即使错误总维度超过半最小距离也能成功译码；两种改进方案在经验上能达到与最近邻译码器相近的插入错误处理能力。

Conclusion: Desarguesian spread码具有超越传统半最小距离界限的译码能力，所提算法为处理插入和删除错误提供了有效的译码方案。

Abstract: Spread codes are a well-known family of constant-dimension subspace-metric codes. For constant dimension $k$ and ambient space dimension $n$ being a multiple of $k$, these codes have minimum distance $2k$ and a rich geometric structure. In this paper, we study the decoding capabilities of the Nearest Neighbor Decoder for Desarguesian spread codes, establishing that unique decoding is still achievable beyond half the minimum distance. Motivated by this, we develop a new decoding algorithm to uniquely decode Desarguesian spread codes in the presence of both insertions and deletions, which increase and decrease, respectively, the dimension of the transmitted codeword. Even when the sum of the dimensions of insertions and deletions exceeds half the minimum distance, provided that deletions are of dimension at most $k-2$, the algorithm succeeds with a small decoding failure. We also propose two refinements to this algorithm that, empirically, can handle nearly as many insertions as the Nearest Neighbor Decoder.

</details>


### [254] [Tight Sample Bounds for Renyi and Min-Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16966)
*Arman Adibi,Piotr Krysta*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了最小熵和Rényi熵（阶数α>1）在固定精度估计下的样本复杂度。最小熵需要Θ(k log k)个样本，比Shannon熵多出Θ(log² k)倍，纠正了此前声称的Θ(k/log k)。对于整数2≤α≤c₀ log k，匹配界为Θ(α k^{1-1/α})，且因子α不可避免。对于实数α≥1.001，得到一致下界Ω(α k^{1-1/α})。在高阶区域，通过最小熵逼近得到Θ(k log k)样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵估计是信息论和性质检验中的基础问题。Shannon熵和最小熵分别是Rényi熵的特例，但二者的样本复杂度存在差异。此前关于最小熵样本复杂度的结果存在错误，且Rényi熵（α>1）的上界和匹配下界尚不完整，因此需要精确刻画。

Method: 对于最小熵上界，使用最大经验频率结合dyadic分组的集中性分析。下界通过在均匀随机位置隐藏一个略重的符号构造。对于Rényi熵，上界使用基于α路碰撞的无偏阶乘下降估计量；下界使用隐蔽重坐标构造。高阶区域通过最小熵逼近Rényi熵（利用0≤H_α-H_∞≤log k/(α-1)）并应用最小熵样本复杂度界。

Result: 最小熵估计的样本复杂度为Θ(k log k)。整数α满足2≤α≤c₀ log k时，Rényi熵样本复杂度为Θ(α k^{1-1/α})，因子α不可避免。实数α≥1.001时，下界为Ω(α k^{1-1/α})。高阶区域（α为log k的大倍数）样本复杂度为Θ(k log k)。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了最小熵和整数阶Rényi熵（α>1）在固定精度下的样本复杂度，纠正了此前错误，并揭示了最小熵与Shannon熵之间Θ(log² k)的样本差距。因子α的不可避免性表明，高阶Rényi熵估计存在固有的复杂度困难。

Abstract: Estimating entropy from samples is fundamental in information theory and property testing. Shannon entropy measures average uncertainty and can be estimated to constant additive accuracy over a $k$-symbol alphabet using $Θ(k/\log k)$ samples. Min-entropy depends only on the most likely symbol. Both are special cases of order-$α$ R'{e}nyi entropy, $H_α$.
  We characterize the sample complexity of estimating min-entropy and R'{e}nyi entropy for $k$ and integer $α>1$; our lower bounds also hold for noninteger $α\ge1.001$. We prove that min-entropy estimation to constant additive accuracy has sample complexity $Θ(k\log k)$. The upper bound uses the largest empirical frequency and concentration via dyadic grouping. The matching lower bound hides a slightly heavier symbol at a uniformly random location. Thus, min-entropy requires $Θ(\log^2 k)$ more samples than Shannon entropy and corrects a previously stated $Θ(k/\log k)$ characterization.
  For every integer $2\leα\le c_0\log k$, we prove the matching fixed-accuracy bound $Θ_{c_0}(αk^{1-1/α})$. Previous results gave $Ω_α(k^{1-1/α})$ for fixed integer $α>1$ and $O_{c_0}(α^2k^{1-1/α})$ for all integer $α>1$. Our upper bound analyzes an unbiased falling-factorial estimator based on $α$-way collisions, while a hidden-heavy-coordinate construction gives the matching lower bound and shows that the factor $α$ is unavoidable. For every real $1.001\leα\le c_0\log k$, we prove the uniform lower bound $Ω_{c_0}(αk^{1-1/α})$. Finally, since $0\le H_α(p)-H_\infty(p)\le\log k/(α-1)$, min-entropy uniformly approximates $H_α$ when $α$ is a sufficiently large multiple of $\log k$. Combining this reduction with our min-entropy bounds gives $Θ_\varepsilon(k\log k)$ sample complexity in the high-order regime.

</details>


### [255] [Transmit Coefficients and Receive Combining Vector Design for OTA-FL with Imperfect CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16983)
*Xiaoyan Ma,Shahryar Zehtabi,Yinan Zou,Taejoon Kim,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对空中计算联邦学习（OTA-FL）中信道状态信息（CSI）不确定导致模型聚合失真问题，通过收敛分析建立时间平均均方误差（MSE）上界，提出联合优化设备端发射系数与参数服务器端接收合并向量的框架，并引入基于李雅普诺夫优化的方法处理因果CSI场景。在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上的实验表明，所提算法能显著降低由不完美CSI引起的测试精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTA-FL虽能提升通信效率，但CSI不确定性会导致全局模型聚合不完美，引入失真并影响最终学习性能。现有研究对长期聚合误差的累积效应及其对训练性能的影响关注不足。

Method: 首先通过收敛分析推导时间平均MSE的上界；然后构建联合优化问题，交替优化发射系数与接收合并向量；针对非因果CSI限制，进一步提出基于李雅普诺夫优化的方法，引入虚拟队列表征长期能耗，实现因果CSI下的发射系数优化。

Result: 在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，所提算法显著降低了由不完美CSI造成的测试精度退化，且性能优于多种基线方案。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化设计和李雅普诺夫方法，可有效缓解CSI不确定性对OTA-FL性能的负面影响，实现长期MSE的最小化。

Abstract: Over-the-air (OTA) computation has recently gained significant attentions as an effective approach to enhance the communication efficiency of wireless federated learning (FL). By enabling simultaneous transmission and aggregation of local model updates, OTA-FL can substantially reduce both latency and bandwidth consumption. However, a key challenge lies in the imperfect aggregation of global models caused by channel state information (CSI) uncertainty, which introduces distortion to the final learning performance. To address this issue, we study the long-term mean squared error (MSE) minimization problem for OTA-FL under imperfect CSI conditions. Through convergence analysis, we establish an upper bound for the time-averaged MSE, thereby revealing the effect of aggregation errors accumulated throughout multiple communication rounds on the overall training performances. Based on this analysis, an optimization framework is developed to minimize the long-term MSE via the joint design of (i) transmit coefficients at the local devices and (ii) receive combining vectors at the parameter server (PS). Since this alternating optimization approach requires non-causal CSI, a Lyapunov-based optimization method is further introduced to handle causal CSI scenarios. By incorporating virtual queues to characterize long-term energy consumption, the proposed method effectively decouples temporal dependencies and allows transmit coefficients to be optimized based on the causal CSI of each aggregation round. Comprehensive evaluations on Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets have demonstrated that the proposed algorithms can significantly reduce the degradation of test accuracy caused by imperfect CSI. Comparisons with other benchmark schemes further verify the superiority of our proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [256] [Cofilling Shattering: A Syndrome-Support Hierarchy for Check Erasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17028)
*Joshua Steier*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二元线性映射的余核（核空间）中，通过释放部分校验坐标（删除校验位）所获得的商空间结构。定义了 Shat_{q,s}(A) 作为最小公共校验支撑，用于区分释放 q 个独立综合征与释放一个无简单线性组合的子空间。证明了其与二进制码的 Griesmer 界、广义 Hamming 重量等概念的联系，并探讨了不同校验基实现下的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图理解在线性编码和同调代数中，当释放（公开）部分校验信息（对应于删除校验坐标）时，所获得的商空间（即释放的商）的结构和度量性质。特别关注如何区分释放独立随机综合征与释放精心选择的有结构子空间两种情况，并刻画其最小重量特性。

Method: 通过定义 Shat_{q,s}(A) 这一组合不变量，建立其与经典编码理论中 Griesmer 界、N_2(q,s)（最短二元码长）的联系。利用“对偶系统”（profile-Griesmer bounds）来独立控制公共校验支撑的大小。通过具体例子（对偶重复码的两种不同实现）展示其对校验基选择的依赖性。最后将结果推广到单纯上边缘映射（simplicial coboundary map）的拓扑语境，与广义 Hamming 重量和 Tutte 多项式建立联系。

Result: 1) 证明了 R_q(C_A) ≥ N_2(q,s) 的有限性下界。2) 对于对偶重复码 C_n={(x,x)}，标准实现 H_0=[I_n I_n] 下 Shat_{q,s}(H_0)=N_2(q,s)；但存在行等价实现使值降为 q。3) 当 s=1 时，该层级结构退化为广义 Hamming 重量且由 Tutte 多项式决定；当 s≥2 时，即使相同标记的割码也可能有不同值。4) 在拓扑语境中，校验删除对应顶面删除，释放的商对应涌现上同调（emergent cohomology）。

Conclusion: Shat_{q,s}(A) 提供了一个精细化的层级结构来刻画校验释放中的安全/信息泄露特性，它超越了传统的广义 Hamming 重量，对校验基的选择敏感，并与经典编码界和拓扑同调有深刻联系。

Abstract: Let $A:\mathbb{F}_2^n\to\mathbb{F}_2^m$ be a binary linear map with fixed coordinate bases, let $C_A=\ker A$, and let $λ_A(y)$ be the minimum Hamming weight of a preimage of the syndrome $y$. We define $\operatorname{Shat}_{q,s}(A)$ as the least common check support of a $q$-dimensional syndrome subspace whose every nonzero element has coset-leader weight at least $s$. It therefore distinguishes release of $q$ independent syndromes from release of a subspace with no easy linear combination. Deleting check coordinates $F$ releases $\ker A_{\bar{F}}/\ker A$, canonically isomorphic to $(\operatorname{im} A)[F]$.
  Finiteness implies $R_q(C_A)\ge \mathsf{N}_2(q,s)$, where $\mathsf{N}_2(q,s)$ is the shortest length of a binary code of dimension $q$ and distance at least $s$; profile-Griesmer bounds independently control common check support. The hierarchy is coordinate-relabeling invariant but can change under a change of check basis. For the pair-repetition code $C_n=\{(x,x):x\in\mathbb{F}_2^n\}$, the standard realization $H_0=[I_n\ I_n]$ has $\operatorname{Shat}_{q,s}(H_0)=\mathsf{N}_2(q,s)$ whenever feasible. For every $q\ge 1$ and $s\ge 2$, with $n=\mathsf{N}_2(q,s)$, a row-equivalent realization of the same code has value $q$.
  For a simplicial coboundary map $A=δ_k$, check erasure is top-face erasure and the released quotient is emergent cohomology. At $s=1$ the hierarchy reduces to generalized Hamming weights and is Tutte-determined; for $s\ge 2$, even identical labeled cut codes can have different values.

</details>


### [257] [Dynamic Channel Knowledge Map: Fundamentals, Construction, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17133)
*Wenjun Jiang,Xiaojun Yuan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出动态信道知识图（Dynamic CKM）的新概念，作为连接准静态环境先验与物理层信号处理的中间层，通过提供时变信道表征来降低6G系统中高维信道的获取开销，并综述了其构建方法、应用场景及开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道知识图（CKM）研究主要聚焦于由建筑物、地形等准静态环境结构塑造的准静态传播特征，未能解决动态散射体、终端姿态变化和射频（RF）损伤引入的时变信道分量问题，亟需一种能够提供时变信道表征的新型框架。

Method: 提出动态CKM概念作为中间层，建立准静态环境先验与物理层信号处理之间的桥梁；综述代表性构建方法；讨论其在导频设计、干扰抑制和通感一体化中的应用。

Result: 动态CKM能够有效支持导频设计、干扰抑制以及集成感知与通信（ISAC），为6G系统提供了一种降低信道获取开销的新架构视角。

Conclusion: 动态CKM是6G系统中的重要架构创新，其与物理层信号处理的协同设计是未来关键研究方向。

Abstract: Wireless communication networks are evolving toward extremely large antenna arrays, millimeter-wave and terahertz bands, and dense heterogeneous deployments, all of which increase channel dimensionality and make channel acquisition increasingly costly. Channel knowledge map (CKM) establishes a mapping from geographical locations to channel characteristics, providing location-specific prior information to reduce the overhead of channel acquisition. Most existing CKM research, however, has focused on quasi-static propagation features shaped by quasi-static environmental structures such as buildings and terrain, leaving unaddressed the time-varying channel component introduced by dynamic scatterers, terminal attitude changes, and radio-frequency (RF) impairments. This article presents a new concept of dynamic CKM as a middle layer that links quasi-static environmental priors to physical-layer signal processing by providing time-evolving channel representations. We first introduce the fundamentals of dynamic CKM, clarifying its relationship with the quasi-static CKM and the physical layer. We then survey representative construction methods and discuss how dynamic CKM can support pilot design, interference suppression, and integrated sensing and communications. Finally, we outline key open research directions in the co-design of dynamic CKM construction and physical-layer signal processing. These discussions offer an architectural perspective on the role of dynamic CKM in emerging 6G systems.

</details>


### [258] [Joint Channel Estimation and Dynamics-Aware Grouping for Time-Varying RIS-Assisted OTA Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17169)
*Ziqi Li,Shuangzhi Li,Uchechukwu Awada,Jiankang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向RIS辅助OTA联邦学习的统一框架，融合GRU时序建模、动态感知用户分组与物理层优化，在不完美CSI和强用户异构条件下提升信道估计精度和聚合鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS辅助OTA-FL中，时变信道、不完美CSI和用户异构性导致聚合精度下降和模型更新抵消，现有方法缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个闭环框架，包含：(1) GRU时序模型捕捉时变CSI演化；(2) OTA联邦聚合与个性化；(3) RIS感知物理层优化；(4) 基于长期路损和短期信道动态的动态感知分组策略。

Result: 在低导频和高移动性场景下，CSI估计精度和OTA聚合性能显著提升；在强用户异构条件下收敛速度和鲁棒性改善。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了RIS辅助OTA-FL在非理想信道和异构环境下的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted over-the-air federated learning (OTA-FL) enables efficient distributed intelligence but suffers from time-varying channels, imperfect channel state information (CSI), and strong user heterogeneity, which jointly degrade aggregation accuracy and cause severe model update cancellation. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework for joint channel estimation and dynamics-aware user grouping in RIS-assisted OTA-FL systems, enabling reliable learning under imperfect CSI and heterogeneous dynamics. The framework integrates gated recurrent unit (GRU) for temporal modeling to capture time-varying CSI evolution, OTA-based federated aggregation with personalization, and RIS-aware physical-layer optimization in a closed loop. In addition, we design a dynamics-aware grouping strategy based on long-term path-loss and short-term channel dynamics to reduce inter-user conflicts under heterogeneous conditions. Simulation results show that the proposed method achieves substantial gains in CSI estimation accuracy and OTA aggregation performance in low-pilot and high-mobility regimes, while improving convergence speed and robustness under strong user heterogeneity.

</details>


### [259] [Compositional Semantic Communication for Physical AI: Category Theory Meets Game Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18115)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Walid Saad,Emilio Calvanese Strinati*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出组合语义通信（CSC）框架，利用范畴论、Grothendieck拓扑和Stackelberg博弈实现异构物理AI源的有效语义表示组合与协调，相比基线方法可实现17%带宽节省和53%端到端延迟降低，同时保持85%推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信方法中，深度学习联合源信道编码存在适应能力有限、分布外泛化差和可扩展性挑战，亟需一种更灵活、可组合的语义通信框架以支持异构物理AI系统的高效协调。

Method: 提出组合语义通信框架：1）基于范畴论度量组合语义，量化设备对推理任务的贡献；2）使用Grothendieck拓扑和预层形式化设备间语义组合；3）将多设备协调建模为Stackelberg博弈，设备提交编码策略，基站最优组合接收的语义表示；4）设计ADMM算法计算均衡信令策略。

Result: 在多样化自动驾驶场景中，相比合作多智能体、分布式梯度下降和均匀选择CSC基线，实现17%带宽减少和53%端到端延迟降低，同时保持85%推理准确率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了所提框架在温和条件下均衡存在且帕累托最优，验证了组合语义通信在物理AI分布式感知系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Physical artificial intelligence (AI) systems involve distributed sensing agents with embedded AI models that must coordinate to perceive, reason, and act in networked environments. Transmitting raw sensor data incurs significant communication overhead, latency, and redundancy. While semantic communication (SC) mitigates these challenges by transmitting task-relevant information, existing deep learning-based joint source-channel coding approaches exhibit limited adaptability, poor out-of-distribution generalization, and scalability challenges. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a framework for compositional semantic communication (CSC), enabling heterogeneous physical AI sources to transmit semantic representations (SRs) that compose meaningfully at a base station (BS) or edge server for remote inference. First, a category-theoretic measure of compositional semantics is developed to quantify each device's contribution to inference tasks beyond mutual information. Second, Grothendieck topologies and presheaves formalize semantic composition across devices, ensuring consistency and task relevance. Building on these foundations, multi-device coordination is formulated as a Stackelberg game in which devices commit to encoding strategies and the BS optimally composes received SRs. An ADMM-based algorithm computes equilibrium signaling strategies. Equilibrium existence is established under mild conditions and is Pareto optimal when compositional information yields increasing collective benefit. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 17% bandwidth reduction and 53% lower end-to-end latency than cooperative multi-agent, distributed gradient descent, and uniform-selection CSC baselines while maintaining 85% inference accuracy across diverse autonomous driving scenarios.

</details>


### [260] [Rate-Distortion-Perception Theory: Redefining the Fundamental Limits of Information Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17232)
*Photios A. Stavrou,Giuseppe Serra,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了率失真感知（RDP）理论，介绍了在损失压缩中引入感知质量作为第三个基本维度的编码原理，并统一了RDP函数的计算优化框架，涵盖离散/连续源、多种感知约束（f-散度、α-散度、Wasserstein度量）以及计算工具（交替最小化、牛顿法、凸优化），同时探讨了高斯源和完美现实主义等可解析情形及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统率失真理论中均方误差等失真度量无法捕捉感知质量或语义有效性，而现代学习驱动应用对感知质量日益重视，因此需要将感知作为第三维度纳入编码框架，以更全面地刻画损失压缩的基本极限。

Method: 本文采用教程综述的方法，首先梳理感知感知损失压缩的编码原理和不同随机性假设下的可达性结果；然后建立RDP函数的统一优化视角，涵盖f-散度、α-散度、Wasserstein度量等广义感知约束，并介绍交替最小化、牛顿法、凸优化等计算工具；最后分析高斯源和完美现实主义等解析情形。

Result: 提供了RDP理论的结构化概述，给出了离散和连续源下多种感知约束的RDP函数计算框架，总结了不同假设下的可达性结果，并指出了可解析求解的特例情形。

Conclusion: RDP理论通过引入感知度量扩展了经典率失真框架，能够更全面地刻画现代压缩系统的性能极限；未来研究方向包括信息论与神经压缩的结合、鲁棒信源编码以及感知感知的网络化控制系统等。

Abstract: Classical rate-distortion (RD) theory has long established the fundamental limits of lossy compression by quantifying the minimum number of bits required to represent a source under a prescribed distortion constraint. However, widely used distortion measures such as mean-squared error often fail to capture perceptual quality or semantic validity, which are increasingly central in modern learning-driven applications. Rate-distortion-perception (RDP) theory extends the RD framework by introducing perception as a third fundamental axis, quantified via distributional similarity between the source and reconstructed signals, leading to the rate-distortion-perception function (RDPF). This tutorial provides a structured overview of the coding principles underlying perception-aware lossy compression and surveys recent achievability results under different randomness assumptions. It then presents a unifying optimization viewpoint for computing the RDPF as defined by Blau and Michaeli, for both discrete and continuous sources under broad families of perceptual constraints, including f-divergences, alpha-divergences, and Wasserstein-based metrics. Special attention is given to computational tools such as alternating minimization schemes, Newton-based methods, and convex optimization formulations, as well as to analytically tractable cases such as Gaussian sources and the perfect-realism regime. Unlike recent broad surveys that emphasize generative architectures and AI-empowered communication systems, this tutorial focuses on the coding-theoretic and computational machinery needed to characterize, compute, and interpret the RDP limits. Finally, the tutorial outlines promising research directions at the intersection of information theory, neural compression, robust source coding, and perception-aware networked control systems.

</details>


### [261] [Critical-Set-Aided Simplified Blind SCL Recognition of Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17480)
*Changwei Tu,Cheng Yang,Xianzhao Feng,Kai Niu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对极化码的盲识别问题，提出了一种基于关键集辅助的简化盲连续消除列表（SBSCL）识别方法，通过在关键集位置进行双假设路径扩展、其余位置保持BSC识别来降低复杂度；同时利用密度演进（DE）优化上下界，在识别成功率和复杂度之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲连续消除列表（BSCL）识别方法在每个信源比特位置都进行双假设路径扩展，导致计算复杂度过高。作者希望通过分析识别错误位置与Bhattacharyya参数上界的关系，找到一种更高效的识别策略来降低复杂度。

Method: 1) 分析了盲连续消除（BSC）识别中首次错误位置与Bhattacharyya参数上界的关系；2) 提出关键集辅助的简化盲连续消除列表（SBSCL）方法，仅在关键集位置进行双假设路径扩展，其余位置保持BSC识别；3) 进一步开发基于密度演进（DE）的上下界，利用理想SC一致条件下的合成对数似然比（LLR）分布计算优化的Chernoff系数（上界）和重叠系数（下界）。

Result: DE-based界比Bhattacharyya参数界更紧，在识别错误概率10^{-2}附近，DE上下界差距在1dB以内；SBSCL与BSCL的识别成功率几乎相同，且关键集大小随SNR增加而快速减小。

Conclusion: SBSCL方法在保持与BSCL几乎相同识别成功率的同时显著降低了复杂度，DE-based界提供了更紧的性能分析。

Abstract: Blind recognition of polar codes from noisy observations is a key problem in non-cooperative signal processing. Although existing blind successive cancellation list (BSCL) recognition exploits channel soft information, it performs two-hypothesis path expansion at every source-bit position, resulting in high complexity. In this paper, we first analyze the first recognition-error positions in the blind successive cancellation (BSC) recognition and observe that they are closely related to the corresponding contribution terms in the existing Bhattacharyya-parameter-based upper bounds. Based on this observation, a critical-set-aided simplified blind successive cancellation list (SBSCL) recognition method is proposed. SBSCL performs two-hypothesis path expansion only at the selected critical-set positions and keeps BSC recognition at the remaining positions, thereby reducing complexity. To improve the reliability of critical-set selection and refine the performance analysis, density-evolution (DE)-based bounds are further developed. Under the ideal SC-consistent condition, the synthetic log-likelihood-ratio (LLR) distributions obtained from density evolution are used to compute the optimized Chernoff coefficient for the upper bound and the overlap coefficient for the lower bound. Simulation results show that the DE-based bounds are tighter than the Bhattacharyya-parameter-based bounds. In the considered settings, the gap between the DE upper and lower bounds is within $1$ dB around a recognition-error probability of $10^{-2}$. Furthermore, SBSCL achieves nearly the same recognition success rate as BSCL, and the size of critical set decreases rapidly as the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) increases.

</details>


### [262] [Generalized BCH Codes and Twisted Goppa Codes Attaining Their Designed Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17517)
*Yaqi Chen,Hao Chen,Cunsheng Ding,Huimin Lao,Chao Liu,Conghui Xie*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义BCH码和扭曲Goppa码的最小距离问题，通过奇偶校验矩阵给出了交替码达到设计距离的充要条件，并证明了一大类广义BCH码和扭曲Goppa码的最小距离等于其设计距离，提供了显式的无穷族而非孤立例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定交替码的真实最小距离是编码理论中一个众所周知的困难问题，本文旨在系统性地研究广义BCH码和扭曲Goppa码的最小距离，以突破现有研究仅限于孤立例子的局限。

Method: 通过奇偶校验矩阵分析，首先给出交替码达到其设计距离的充要条件，然后将该条件应用于广义BCH码和扭曲Goppa码。对于扭曲Goppa码Γ(L,g,η)（deg g = t），给出了d(Γ(L,g,η)) = t+1的刻划条件。

Result: 证明了一大类广义BCH码的最小距离等于其设计距离，并给出了显式的无穷族；同时刻划了扭曲Goppa码最小距离达到t+1的条件，导出了达到该距离的结构化类和无穷族。

Conclusion: 本文为广义BCH码和扭曲Goppa码的最小距离问题提供了系统性结果，填补了从孤立例子到无穷族的研究空白，对编码理论中交替码距离分析有重要推进。

Abstract: Determining the true minimum distance of an alternant code remains a notoriously difficult problem in coding theory. In this paper, we study the minimum distances of generalized BCH codes and twisted Goppa codes through their parity-check matrices. We first give a necessary and sufficient condition for an alternant code to attain its designed distance and apply it to generalized BCH codes. As applications, we prove that broad classes of generalized BCH codes have minimum distances equal to their designed distances. These classes provide explicit infinite families rather than isolated examples. We characterize when a twisted Goppa code $Γ(L,g,η)$ with $\operatorname{deg} g=t$ satisfies $d(Γ(L,g,η))=t+1$, and derive structured classes and infinite families attaining this distance.

</details>


### [263] [Enhanced ODMA for Massive Sparse Access: Hybrid Data-Frozen Bit Transmission and Fixed-Point Analysis over Block Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17553)
*Guanghui Song,Jianxiang Yan,Ying Li,Zhaoji Zhang,Jun Cheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型的开关分多址（ODMA）传输方案，通过在码字中插入少量冻结符号实现高效的多用户联合信道估计与迭代译码，在300用户系统中仅需5~20个符号即可达到与完美信道估计相当的性能，并提出定点分析方法可准确估计迭代收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于导频的信道估计方法在多用户大规模系统中需要大量导频开销，而现有方法存在复杂度高或性能不足的问题，亟需一种既能高效估计信道又能辅助译码的低复杂度方案。

Method: 在码字中稀疏插入少量冻结符号（兼具导频和译码辅助功能），采用低复杂度单用户信道估计与译码结合简单迭代干扰消除，并针对大规模ODMA系统提出定点分析方法，仅利用单用户AWGN信道下的译码函数即可估计多用户块衰落信道下的迭代收敛性能。

Result: 仅需每用户5~20个冻结符号（300用户系统）即可达到与完美已知用户信道等效的性能；定点分析方法能准确估计迭代收敛性能，无需大量蒙特卡洛仿真。

Conclusion: 该方案在极低开销下实现了高效的多用户联合信道估计与译码，定点分析为任意码长、码率和译码算法的多用户码设计提供了高效工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel on-off division multiple access (ODMA) transmission scheme that enables efficient joint multi-user channel estimation and iterative decoding by inserting a small number of frozen symbols into the codewords. Functionally analogous to pilots, these symbols are sparsely distributed within the codeword. Unlike conventional pilot-based methods, our approach requires only a minimal number of frozen symbols (e.g., $5\sim20$ symbols per user in a 300-user system), which serve dual purposes as both estimation references and decoding aids. By employing low-complexity single-user channel estimation and decoding, combined with simple iterative interference cancellation, the scheme achieves performance equivalent to that with perfectly known user channels, even when accounting for the additional energy and bandwidth costs of the frozen symbols. Furthermore, for the large-scale ODMA sparse multiple access system, this paper proposes a fixed-point analysis method, which can accurately estimate the iterative convergence performance over multi-user block fading channels by only leveraging the decoding functions under the single-user AWGN channel. This method is applicable to performance analysis for arbitrary code lengths, code rates, and decoding algorithms. It eliminates the need for extensive Monte Carlo simulation time, and provides an efficient tool for the design of multi-user codes.

</details>


### [264] [Skew CRT codes and their decoding in poly skew metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17737)
*Kayode Epiphane Nouetowa,Olivier Ruatta*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文引入斜CRT码和多斜度量及相关错误模型，并为此类码在多斜度量下提供译码算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有编码理论中特定错误模型的局限性，引入新的码族和度量标准以应对更复杂的错误模式。

Method: 定义了斜CRT码（skew CRT codes）和多斜度量（poly-skew metric），并基于该度量设计了相应的译码算法。

Result: 成功构建了斜CRT码族，并给出了在多斜度量下的有效译码算法。

Conclusion: 斜CRT码在多斜度量下具有可译码性，拓展了编码理论在非标准度量下的应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce skew CRT codes and the poly-skew metric with associated error model, and we provide a decoding algorithm for this family of codes under the poly-skew metric.

</details>


### [265] [Autonomous Discovery of Wireless Communications Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17762)
*Fayçal Aït Aoudia,Jakob Hoydis,Sebastian Cammerer,Gian Marti,Merlin Nimier-David,Nicolas Roussel,Alexander Keller*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: AITE是一个利用大模型驱动进化搜索来自动设计无线通信算法的框架，在OTFS均衡器和OFDM无导频接收机两个任务上取得了超越现有方法的效果，其中OTFS均衡延迟降低3.6倍，OFDM接收机首次实现了与神经接收机性能相当的显式可解释算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型驱动的进化搜索已在多个科学领域展示了算法发现能力，但在无线通信领域的应用尚属空白，本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 提出AITE框架，利用大语言模型驱动进化搜索，在性能-复杂度权衡下自主设计通信算法，应用于OTFS均衡器和OFDM无导频接收机两个物理层问题。

Result: 1) OTFS均衡器：超越已知最优方案，计算延迟降低3.6倍；2) OFDM接收机：首次发现首个显式、可解释的算法，性能达到与最先进神经接收机相当的水平。

Conclusion: 大模型驱动的进化搜索在下一代无线通信算法的自主发现方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven evolutionary search is an emerging algorithm-discovery paradigm that has already produced novel results in several scientific fields. Yet its application to wireless communications remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce The AI Telco Engineer (AITE), a framework to autonomously design algorithms for complex communication problems, while navigating performance-complexity tradeoffs. We showcase AITE on two challenging physical-layer problems: designing an equalizer for an orthogonal time-frequency space (OTFS) system, and constructing a receiver algorithm for an orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) system using a custom constellation and operating without pilots. For the first task, AITE develops algorithms that outperform the best-known solutions while reducing computational latency by a factor of 3.6 compared to the strongest baseline. For the second task, it discovers the first explicit, explainable algorithms that achieve performance parity with state-of-the-art neural receivers. These results demonstrate the strong potential of LLM-driven evolutionary search for the autonomous discovery of next-generation wireless communications algorithms.

</details>


### [266] [Task-Oriented Precoding for Edge Inference over Large-Scale MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17877)
*Hongru Li,Zeyan Zhuang,Zixin Wang,Hengtao He,Shenghui Song,Jun Zhang,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机矩阵理论的统计预编码框架，利用信道协方差和特征统计信息设计慢时标MIMO预编码器，避免了对瞬时信道状态信息的依赖，通过最大化接收特征的类可分性（MCR²）来优化任务导向的分布式推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务导向预编码方法依赖瞬时信道状态信息（CSIT），在多设备MIMO系统中，获取聚合信道、反馈CSI或优化预编码器以及跨相干块重新优化会带来巨大开销。因此需要一种仅基于长期统计信息（而非瞬时CSIT）的慢时标预编码设计方法。

Method: 采用最大编码率降低（MCR²）作为任务感知效用函数来衡量接收特征的类可分性；利用随机矩阵理论推导出该效用的确定性近似，将其转化为仅依赖于长期统计量和系统维度比的固定点目标函数；提出投影块坐标上升与逐次凸逼近算法，在每设备功率约束下优化该确定性目标。

Result: 在ModelNet10数据集上的实验验证了确定性近似的准确性，并表明所提出的统计预编码器在任务感知模式分配和推理性能上优于竞争基准方法。

Conclusion: 基于统计CSIT的慢时标预编码设计是可行的，能够在不依赖瞬时信道信息的情况下有效提升多设备MIMO系统中分布式推理的任务性能，为任务导向的物理层优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Future wireless networks are expected to support networked artificial intelligence (AI) services, where multiple devices transmit learned features to an edge server for distributed inference. This setting calls for task-oriented physical-layer optimization, where wireless transmission should preserve useful information for inference rather than only maximize the rate or reconstruct the transmitted signals. A key physical-layer control variable is the multiple-input multiple-output precoder, which determines how device features are shaped and combined over wireless channels. Existing task-oriented precoding methods typically adapt the precoder to instantaneous channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT). However, in multi-device MIMO systems, acquiring the aggregate channel, feeding back CSI or optimized precoders, and reoptimizing across coherence blocks introduce substantial overhead. This paper develops a random-matrix-theoretic framework based on statistical CSIT that designs a slow-timescale precoder from channel covariance statistics and training-set feature statistics, without requiring instantaneous CSIT. We adopt maximal coding rate reduction (MCR${^2}$) to measure the class separability of the received features, yielding a task-aware utility for MIMO precoder design. Since this utility still depends on random small-scale fading, we derive a deterministic approximation that converts it into a fixed-point objective depending only on long-term statistics and large-system dimension ratios via random matrix theory. A projected block-coordinate ascent and successive convex approximation algorithm is developed to optimize this deterministic objective under per-device power constraints. Experiments on ModelNet10 verify the approximation and show that the proposed statistical precoder improves task-aware mode allocation and inference performance over competitive benchmarks.

</details>


### [267] [Optimal Domain-Aware Privacy Mechanisms for Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18021)
*Sajani Vithana,Sangwon Jung,Haoyang Hu,Viveck R. Cadambe,Flavio P. Calmon,Haewon Jeong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了PubMix，一种将公共数据直接整合到差分隐私机制中的方法，通过理论上最优的域感知隐私机制，显著提升了合成数据生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私合成数据方法虽然利用公共数据改善隐私-保真度权衡，但仅通过预处理或后处理间接使用公共数据，依赖域无关的DP机制。本文旨在建立将公共数据原则性整合到DP机制本身的理论框架。

Method: 将归一化直方图作为分布估计器，在特定DP机制类别内刻画渐近最优的域感知隐私机制；提出PubMix——一种公共数据感知的DP机制，可用于基于直方图的数据合成流程。

Result: 实验表明，PubMix在合成数据生成质量上显著优于域无关的隐私机制。

Conclusion: 将公共数据直接融入DP机制本身（而非仅作为预处理/后处理步骤）能够带来实质性的性能提升，为隐私保护数据合成提供了新的理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) imposes fundamental trade-offs between privacy and statistical fidelity in synthetic data generation. While access to public data has been shown to improve these trade-offs empirically, existing approaches use public data only indirectly, through pre-processing (e.g., using pre-trained generative models) or post-processing steps (e.g., matching target statistics estimated from public datasets), while relying on domain-agnostic DP mechanisms. In this work, we lay the theoretical framework to study the principled incorporation of public data into DP mechanisms themselves. We consider normalized histograms as distribution estimators and characterize the asymptotically optimal domain-aware privacy mechanism within a specific class of DP mechanisms. We introduce PubMix, a public-data-aware DP mechanism that can be used in histogram-based data synthesis pipelines. Our experiments demonstrate that PubMix significantly improves synthetic data generation quality compared to domain-agnostic privacy mechanisms.

</details>


### [268] [When Phase Doesn't Matter: Self-Coherent Over-the-Air Computation at Sub-THz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18052)
*Sherif Ghozzy,Mohamed Seif,H. Vincent Poor,Kaushik Sengupta*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于克拉默斯-克朗尼格（KK）接收的自相干无合成器空中计算框架，通过偏置聚合波形和直接检测结合KK相位重建，消除了对载波同步和相干接收的依赖，降低了多用户函数聚合中的同步开销与复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空中计算（OAC）依赖精确载波同步和相干接收，这在短距离、低复杂度系统中成本高昂且脆弱，尤其在毫米波和亚太赫兹频段，振荡器相位不稳定性成为可扩展相干OAC的根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出自相干无合成器OAC框架：发射端发送偏置聚合波形，接收端采用直接检测+KK相位重建，替代载波恢复和相干接收；建立多用户KK接收下的信号域系统模型，进行同步松弛分析。

Result: 理论分析证明该架构从根本上消除了发射机与接收机之间的载波频偏（CFO）敏感性；给出了每个符号的均方误差（MSE）表征，表明自相干架构在实际条件下逼近基带OAC的理论性能极限。

Conclusion: 该框架特别适用于毫米波和亚太赫兹系统，能有效克服振荡器相位不稳定性对可扩展相干OAC的限制，降低控制开销并提升多用户聚合的可扩展性。

Abstract: Over-the-air computation (OAC) enables efficient function aggregation in wireless networks by exploiting the superposition property of the multiple-access channel. However, practical deployment of OAC is severely challenged by the reliance on accurate carrier synchronization and coherent reception, which are costly and fragile, especially in short-range and low-complexity systems. In this work, we propose a \emph{self-coherent, synthesizer-free over-the-air computation framework} based on \emph{Kramers--Kronig (KK) reception}. By transmitting a biased aggregate waveform and employing direct detection followed by KK phase reconstruction at the receiver, the proposed scheme eliminates the need for explicit carrier recovery while preserving coherent-like signal aggregation.
  We develop a signal-domain system model for multi-user OAC under KK reception and provide a synchronization-relaxation analysis demonstrating that the proposed architecture fundamentally removes carrier-frequency offset (CFO) sensitivity between transmitters and receiver. By shifting synchronization complexity away from strict carrier-phase tracking and eliminating distributed phase alignment requirements, the framework reduces control overhead and improves scalability in multi-user aggregation. A detailed per-symbol mean-squared error (MSE) characterization isolates the impact of channel mismatch and KK reconstruction noise, showing that the proposed self-coherent architecture approaches the theoretical performance limits of baseband OAC under practical operating conditions. Finally, we demonstrate that the approach is particularly well suited for mmWave and sub-THz systems, where oscillator phase instability otherwise represents a fundamental bottleneck to scalable coherent OAC.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [Audited Auctions: Reducing Harms in Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16586)
*Tejovan Parker,Gabriel McDonnell Maayan,Francisco Marmolejo-Cossío,Marshall Van Alstyne*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种审计与惩罚机制，帮助拍卖者筛选出最严重的负外部性，从而在广告市场中提升社会福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准拍卖机制未能考虑竞标分配对非竞标者造成的外部性，可能导致无限制的损害。以广告市场为代表，用户注意力被买卖，亟需机制纠正这种外部性。

Method: 提出一种由拍卖者实施的审计与惩罚机制，旨在筛选并抑制最严重的外部性。

Result: 从理论上证明了该机制能够内化外部性，并通过实证研究显示了社会福利的改进。

Conclusion: 审计与惩罚机制能够有效降低拍卖带来的负外部性，提升整体社会福利。

Abstract: Although standard auction mechanisms help truthfully reveal preferences of bidders, they can inadvertently result in unbounded harms when they fail to account for externalities caused by bid allocations affecting non-bidders. Ad markets, that buy and sell user attention represent such auctions. This research explores a welfare improving auctioneer's audit-and-penalty mechanism that helps screen the worst externalities. We prove this mechanism can internalize externalities formally, then explore social welfare gains empirically.

</details>


### [270] [Censorship Resistance and Throughput with Multiple Concurrent Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16995)
*Aditya Saraf,Giannis Kaklamanis,Sarisht Wadhwa,Fatima Elsheimy*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了区块链中的经济审查阻力（eCR），定义了对抗性贿赂提议者排除目标交易的成本与用户支付比例。研究发现单一提议者系统在首价拍卖和费用销毁机制下结构脆弱，而多并发提议者（MCP）通过增加贿赂成本提升审查阻力，但会引入交易重复降低吞吐量。作者建立博弈模型求解混合均衡，分析三种交易费用机制（TFM），表明惩罚重复的TFM在多数场景下最优，并用以太坊数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链声称核心优势是抗审查，但实践中提议者因法律、经济等原因频繁审查交易。现有研究缺乏对经济激励型审查的系统性量化分析，尤其缺乏对多提议者并发机制下审查成本与吞吐量之间权衡的理论理解。

Method: 定义经济审查阻力（eCR）为对抗性成功审查的期望成本除以用户期望支付。建立博弈论模型，考虑共享mempool中验证者构建区块受对抗性贿赂影响，设计算法求解混合策略均衡（每个交易被包含的概率）。通过仿真分析不同提议者数量和三种TFM（包括惩罚重复的TFM）下的eCR和吞吐量，并用以太坊实证数据验证。

Result: 单提议者系统下eCR仅为用户支付的百分之几，结构脆弱。多并发提议者（MCP）能显著提升审查成本，但引入交易重复降低吞吐量。惩罚重复交易的TFM在大多数设置下优于其他两种TFM。仿真结果与以太坊实证数据一致。

Conclusion: 多并发提议者机制通过增加贿赂所需收买的提议者数量来提升经济审查阻力，但需在审查阻力和吞吐量之间权衡；惩罚重复交易的费用机制能有效缓解重复问题，是最优选择。

Abstract: Censorship resistance is the defining advantage of blockchains over their centralized counterparts. Yet block proposers censor transactions for many reasons, from legal consequences to economic incentives. We study economically-incentivized censorship, modeled by an adversary who bribes proposers to exclude a target transaction, and define the economic censorship resistance (eCR) of a transaction as the adversary's expected cost of successful censorship divided by the user's expected payment for inclusion. Single-proposer systems are structurally weak by this measure: under a first-price auction the adversary need only match the user's bid, and fee burning pushes eCR to a few percent of what the user pays.
  We therefore turn to multiple concurrent proposers (MCP), where block capacity is divided among $n$ proposers and the block is the union of their sub-blocks. While MCP can substantially increase the cost of censorship by requiring the adversary to bribe many proposers, it also introduces transaction duplication, reducing throughput. The resulting trade-off depends critically on the transaction fee mechanism (TFM), which determines how fees are shared among competing proposers.
  We create a game theoretic model where validators construct blocks from a shared mempool, subject to an adversary's bribery attempt. We provide an algorithm that solves for the mixed equilibrium of a given mempool, which is characterized by the probability of including each transaction. This algorithm works for a wide class of TFMs, and allows us to calculate the expected throughput and censorship resistance for any bid distribution. We then use simulations to show how the eCR and throughput vary as the number of proposers increases. We compare three TFMs, finding that the duplication-penalizing TFM dominates the others across many settings. We also validate our findings with empirical Ethereum data.

</details>


### [271] [Equilibrium analysis of three-player General Lotto game with leader-follower framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17062)
*Yang Jiao,Dunbiao Niu,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了带监管者的General Lotto博弈（R-Lotto），研究监管者先选择干预参数，然后两个资源受限的追随者进行带有偏好的General Lotto子博弈。推导了追随者的均衡收益，刻画了监管者的Nash-Stackelberg均衡干预策略，并进一步扩展到多战场场景下带预算约束的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有General Lotto博弈主要关注两个竞争者的直接对抗，缺少对监管干预这一重要现实场景的建模。作者旨在通过引入领导-追随者结构，刻画监管者如何通过策略性干预影响竞争性资源分配。

Method: 采用Stackelberg博弈框架构建R-Lotto模型：监管者作为领导者先选择干预参数，追随者随后进行带有偏好的General Lotto子博弈。利用均衡分析方法推导追随者收益函数和监管者的最优干预策略。在多战场版本中引入监管者预算约束，并通过数值仿真验证。

Result: 推导出了追随者在带有偏好的一般Lotto子博弈中的均衡收益，并刻画了监管者的Nash-Stackelberg均衡干预策略。在多战场场景下，追随者子博弈在不同战场间解耦，而监管者的干预决策通过共同预算耦合。数值仿真验证了均衡特征并为监管者提供了决策指导。

Conclusion: R-Lotto模型成功将监管干预引入竞争性资源分配博弈，为理解监管者如何通过策略性干预（带有预算约束）影响竞争结果提供了理论框架和量化分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the General Lotto game with a regulator (R-Lotto), a leader-follower extension of the classical two-player General Lotto game. The model captures regulatory interventions in competitive resource allocation, where a regulator first chooses an intervention parameter to influence the subsequent competition between two resource-constrained followers. The intervention parameter represents favoritism toward one of the followers, and the followers then play a general Lotto subgame with favoritism. We derive the followers' equilibrium payoff and characterize the Nash--Stackelberg equilibrium (NSE) intervention of the regulator. We further develop a multi-battlefield R-Lotto model with a regulator budget constraint. In this setting, the follower subgames on different battlefield is decoupled, while the regulator's intervention decisions are coupled through a common budget. Numerical simulations demonstrate the proposed equilibrium characterizations and provide practical decision-making guidance for the regulator.

</details>


### [272] [Temporal Fair Division of Indivisible Goods with Structured Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17224)
*Kui-Wang Choi,Minming Li*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究时间公平分配问题，提出动态回溯框架在强二值估值下实现精确TEFX，并通过lexicographical势函数论证证明算法有限步终止，同时建立了α-TEFX在相同估值和双值物品下的紧近似比，以及TEFX和TMMS的不可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平分配研究多聚焦单轮分配，而现实中的物品分配常跨多个时间轮次进行，智能体对累积公平性有长期需求。已知严格的时间公平标准（TEFX、TMMS）通常无法满足，因此需要在约束条件下刻画可行与不可行的边界。

Method: 提出一种新颖的动态回溯框架（dynamic backtracking framework），通过有限的历史重分配（bounded historical reallocation）系统性解决时间嫉妒循环；利用lexicographical势函数论证（lexicographical potential function argument）证明算法有限步终止；结合近似比分析与不可能性证明，系统性刻画时间公平概念的结构边界。

Result: 在强二值估值下实现了精确TEFX；建立了α-TEFX在相同估值和双值物品下的紧近似比；获得TEFX和TMMS的针对性不可能性结果，明确了时间公平在数学上不可达的确切条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统地图谱了时间公平分配的结构边界，所提动态回溯框架是首个在非平凡估值类上实现精确TEFX的算法，同时通过紧近似比和不可能性结果为准确定位先前算法提供了理论基准。

Abstract: This paper investigates temporal fair division, a setting where items are allocated over multiple rounds and agents require cumulative fairness over time. We focus on dynamic extensions of classic fairness notions: Temporal Envy-Freeness Up to Any Good (TEFX), its $α$-TEFX approximation, and Temporal Maximin Share (TMMS). Because these strict fairness criteria are known to be generally impossible to satisfy, we analyze the model under constraints to map the boundary between what is possible and what is not.
  Our contribution systematically maps the structural boundaries of these temporal fairness notions. Our main technical result introduces a novel dynamic backtracking framework that achieves exact TEFX for Strong Binary Valuations. We prove, via a lexicographical potential function argument, that this bounded historical reallocation systematically resolves temporal envy cycles and terminates in finite time. Finally, we establish tight approximation ratios for $α$-TEFX under identical valuations and bi-valued goods, alongside targeted impossibility results for TEFX and TMMS, demonstrating exactly where temporal fairness is mathematically unattainable, and properly contextualizing prior algorithms that only implicitly satisfied our newly defined TMMS metric.

</details>


### [273] [When One Good Is Not Enough: EF1 and Pareto Optimality Are Not Compatible for Submodular Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17811)
*Simon Mackenzie,Mashbat Suzuki*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于次模估值，EF1（至多一件物品无嫉妒）与帕累托最优性（PO）不可兼得，否定了该领域一个重要的开放性问题。文章进一步刻画了这种不可兼得的边界：加权拟阵秩估值下EF1与分数帕累托最优性（fPO）也不兼容，但识别了一种“公共包络”条件可恢复兼容性，并量化了坚持EF1时不可避免的效率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答离散公平分配中的一个核心开放问题：次模估值下EF1（公平性）与PO（效率）是否总能同时实现？该问题由Caragiannis等人提出，是公平分配领域的重要未解难题。

Method: 构造反例（2个智能体、8个物品、次模估值）证明EF1与弱PO不可兼得；利用加权拟阵秩估值证明EF1与fPO不兼容；提出“公共包络”条件恢复兼容性；通过下界分析量化EF1下的效率损失。

Result: （1）次模估值下EF1与PO不可兼得；（2）加权拟阵秩估值下EF1与fPO不兼容；（3）“公共包络”条件（如公共权重拟阵秩估值）下EF1+PO总存在；（4）次模反例存在常数α<1使无EF1分配是α-PO；次可加估值下紧界为1/√2。

Conclusion: 次模估值打破了加性估值下EF1与PO兼容的美好性质，但通过公共包络条件可部分恢复兼容性；效率损失的下界量化了公平与效率之间的张力。

Abstract: One of the central questions in discrete fair division is whether fairness and efficiency can be achieved simultaneously. For indivisible goods, a canonical relaxation of envy-freeness is envy-freeness up to one good (EF1), while the standard efficiency benchmark is Pareto optimality (PO). In their seminal work, Caragiannis et al. showed that, for additive valuations, EF1 and PO are always compatible, and asked whether this compatibility extends to submodular valuations. This question has since become an important open problem in the study of fair division.
  In this paper, we settle the question in the negative. We construct an instance with two agents and eight goods, where both agents have submodular valuations, such that no EF1 allocation is even weakly Pareto optimal. Thus, the celebrated compatibility between EF1 and PO for additive valuations breaks down already for two agents under submodular valuations.
  We then map the boundary of this impossibility. On the negative side, we show that even for weighted matroid rank valuations, EF1 and fractional Pareto optimality (fPO) are incompatible. This rules out, in general, broad classes of weighted-welfare and Fisher-market-based approaches. On the positive side, we identify a common-envelope condition that restores compatibility. Under this condition, EF1+PO allocations exist for any number of agents. This yields new positive results showing that common-weight matroid-rank valuations always admit EF1+PO allocations.
  Finally, we quantify the efficiency loss that is unavoidable when insisting on EF1. Our submodular counterexample implies that there is a constant $α<1$ such that no EF1 allocation is $α$-\PO. For the broader class of subadditive valuations, we prove a tight two-agent bound: for any $\varepsilon>0$, there exists an instance in which no EF1 allocation is $\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\varepsilon\right)$-PO.

</details>


### [274] [Beyond Stability: Improved Efficiency Guarantees for $α$-Stable Matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17949)
*Isabel Fernandez Abad,Sophie Klumper,Guido Schäfer*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了稳定匹配中稳定性与效率之间的权衡，提出了α-稳定性的概念，并在非对称估值下给出了完整的刻画。通过一种多项式时间算法，在α ≤ μ/(μ+1)时实现完全效率，否则达到(1/α)·μ/(μ+1)的最优社会福利保证。同时证明了求解最优α-稳定匹配是NP难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定匹配机制在稳定性与社会福利最优性之间存在固有张力，传统稳定匹配可能牺牲过多效率。本文旨在通过松弛稳定性条件（引入α-稳定性）来探索稳定性与效率之间的权衡，并在非对称估值下给出完整刻画。

Method: 本文定义了α-稳定性（只有当收益足够大时才会偏离），引入了非对称程度参数μ刻画估值不对称性。提出了一种多项式时间算法：先对一个最优匹配的估值进行膨胀处理，然后在该修改后的实例上运行Gale-Shapley算法。同时通过NP-hardness证明分析了该问题的计算复杂性边界。

Result: 当α ≤ μ/(μ+1)时，算法可实现完全效率（1-效率）；当α更大时，可实现至少(1/α)·μ/(μ+1)的最优社会福利保证。计算最优α-稳定匹配是NP难的，即使α接近1时也是如此。

Conclusion: 适当放松稳定性可以显著提升效率保证，文中算法达到了理论上可达的最优效率保证。稳定性与效率的权衡由非对称程度μ和松弛参数α共同决定，且这种权衡的计算复杂性存在本质困难。

Abstract: Stable matching mechanisms are fundamental to market design but face an inherent tension between stability and social welfare optimality. We study a natural relaxation of stability, termed $α$-stability, which models agents as willing to deviate only when the potential improvement is sufficiently large. Under $α$-stability, no pair of agents can deviate and improve their valuations by more than a factor of $1/α$, with $α\in (0,1]$. We provide a complete characterization of the stability-efficiency tradeoff under asymmetric valuations. This tradeoff depends on the degree of asymmetry $μ\in (0,1]$, which bounds the ratio between agents' valuations for any pair. Our results show that relaxing stability can substantially improve achievable efficiency guarantees. We further present a polynomial-time algorithm that computes an $α$-stable matching attaining the best possible efficiency guarantee. For $α\le μ/(μ+1)$, our algorithm achieves 1-efficiency; for larger $α$, it computes an $α$-stable matching achieving at least $(1/α)\cdot μ/(μ+1)$ of the optimal social welfare. Remarkably, our algorithm inflates the values of an optimal matching and then applies the Gale-Shapley algorithm to the modified instance. Finally, we show that computing an optimal $α$-stable matching is NP-hard, even under slight relaxations of stability, i.e., for $α$ close to 1.

</details>


### [275] [Nonexistence of Simultaneously EF1 and Pareto Optimal Allocations for Submodular Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18220)
*Harish Chandramouleeswaran,Prajakta Nimbhorkar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明在单调子模 valuations（甚至未加权覆盖 valuations）下，两个代理人的不可分物品分配中，同时满足 EF1 和 PO 的分配不一定存在，且判断其存在性是 NP-hard 的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于单调子模 valuations 下是否存在同时满足 EF1（无嫉妒至多一件物品）和 PO（帕累托最优）的分配，这一直是一个长期未解决的开放问题。

Method: 构造了两个代理人的反例，使用未加权覆盖 valuations（单调子模 valuations 的严格子类），证明不存在 EF1+PO 的分配；并通过归约证明判定该存在性问题是 NP-hard 的。

Result: 1. 在单调子模 valuations 下，两个代理人的场景中 EF1+PO 分配不一定存在（给出了反例）。2. 判定 EF1+PO 分配的存在性是 NP-hard 问题。3. 该反例同样适用于家务（chores）场景（将 valuations 解释为负效用）。

Conclusion: 将 EF1+PO 分配不存在的边界从单调子加 valuations 推进到了未加权覆盖 valuations，填补了已知存在性结果（加性 valuations 和拟阵秩 valuations）与不存在性结果之间的空白。

Abstract: The existence of allocations of indivisible goods that are simultaneously fair (envy-free up to one item (EF1)) and efficient (Pareto optimal (PO)) when agents have monotone submodular valuations has been a longstanding open problem. We settle this question negatively by giving an example with two agents where no allocation is simultaneously EF1 and PO. We also show that determining the existence of such allocations is NP-hard for monotone submodular valuations. Our example uses (unweighted) coverage valuations, which is a strict subclass of monotone submodular valuations. Since EF1+PO allocations are known to always exist for additive valuations via the maximization of Nash Social Welfare (Caragiannis et al. (ACM TEAC 2019)), and for matroid-rank valuations (Benabbou et al. (ACM TEAC 2021)), nonexistence was known only for monotone subadditive valuations (Caragiannis et al. (ACM TEAC 2019)). Our work moves the nonexistence frontier to unweighted coverage valuations. We also show that the example we designed for goods also proves nonexistence of EF1+PO in general, for chores with unweighted coverage costs, by interpreting the valuations as disutilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [On the Performance and Implementation of Parallax free Video See-Through Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16484)
*Ricardo Augusto Borsoi,Guilherme Holsbach Costa*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种用于透视系统的视频透视方法，通过双目摄像头和特征检测算法估计用户、显示器和背景场景的相对位置，并基于平面场景模型补偿视差误差，应用于驾驶员辅助系统。理论评估表明，尽管采用了平面模型和固定工作距离等简化假设，但在某些实际场景中这些近似对感知质量影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在透视系统中，显示器遮挡部分背景场景，需要补偿图像采集系统带来的视差误差和其他畸变，以实现透明效果。本文旨在提供一种带有视差校正的视频透视方法的详细研究。

Method: 采用双摄像头系统（一个朝向用户，一个朝向背景场景），通过特征检测算法估计用户、显示器和场景之间的相对位置，基于平面场景模型补偿视差误差，并应用于驾驶员辅助系统。

Result: 理论评估表明，尽管采用了平面场景模型和固定工作距离等简化近似以降低计算成本，但在某些实际情况下，这些近似对感知质量的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提出的视频透视方法在简化假设下仍能保持可接受的感知质量，适用于驾驶员辅助系统等实际场景。

Abstract: In see-through systems an observer watches a (background) scene partially occluded by a display. In this display, usually positioned close to the observer, a region of the background scene is shown, yielding the sensation that the display is transparent. To achieve the transparency effect, it is very important to compensate the parallax error and other distortions caused by the image acquisition system. In this paper a detailed study of a video see-through methodology with parallax correction is performed. In a system composed by two cameras -- one directed to the user and another to the background scene -- and a display, the relative position between the user, the display and the scene is estimated using a feature detection algorithm and the parallax error is compensated assuming a planar scene model. The application of the proposed methodology on Driver Assistance Systems (DAS) is proposed. A theoretical assessment of the algorithm shows that although approximations are proposed to simplify the methodology and reduce the computational cost, such as the planar scene model and fixed working distance, on some practical situations their effects can be neglected without noticeable impact on the perceptual quality of the solution.

</details>


### [277] [Points as Tori: Fast Pointwise Signed Distance for Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16946)
*Nicole Feng,Ioannis Gkioulekas,Keenan Crane*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于圆环面局部拟合的点云有符号距离计算方法，无需全局优化或空间离散化，支持任意分辨率下的快速逐点查询，并统一了有符号距离与绕数法、泊松曲面重建的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法计算点云有符号距离通常需要全局优化、空间离散化或显式曲面重建，计算成本高且难以并行化。本文旨在开发一种可直接对点云进行快速、任意分辨率有符号距离查询的方法，无需中间重建步骤。

Method: 利用预训练网络逐点输出曲率和偏移参数，在局部用具有闭合形式有符号距离函数的圆环面拟合点云，以前馈方式完成重建和距离计算。方法在理论上统一了有符号距离与绕数法、泊松曲面重建。

Result: 在摄影测量点云、网格、3D高斯和神经隐式表示等多种来源的点云上验证了有效性。支持点云偏移、形态学操作、布尔运算和球体追踪直接可视化。

Conclusion: 该方法无需显式曲面重建即可直接在点云上应用多种几何处理任务，效率高且易于并行化，为点云处理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We describe a method for computing signed distance to point clouds that allows fast pointwise evaluation at arbitrary spatial resolution. As input, our method takes a point cloud with normals; as output, it provides an analytical parameterization that allows queries of signed distance to the approximate underlying surface at arbitrary points - simultaneously providing reconstruction and distance. Our key idea is to reconstruct shapes by locally fitting point clouds with tori, which have closed-form signed distance functions. Tori are fitted in a feed-forward manner, using a pre-trained network to output per-point curvature and shift parameters. Importantly, our method does not require costly global optimization or spatial discretization, and is easily parallelizable. Underlying our method is a new theory that unifies signed distance with the classic reconstruction methods of winding numbers and Poisson surface reconstruction. We use our method to compute signed distance to point clouds arising from photogrammetry, meshes, 3D Gaussians, and neural implicits. Our method allows point clouds to be used directly in applications, without explicit surface reconstruction: as examples, we take offsets of point clouds, apply morphological and Boolean operations, and directly visualize offset surfaces using sphere tracing.

</details>


### [278] [Text2Villa: Hierarchical Generation of 3D Indoor Environments with Physics-Aware Analysis-by-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17145)
*Xiang Tang,Ruotong Li,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Text2Villa提出了一种层次化生成框架，通过宏观层面的多楼层布局生成和微观层面的物理语义场景图约束，结合闭环优化和碰撞检测，实现了从自然语言生成高保真、物理合理的别墅级3D室内场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法生成具有垂直连通性和任意多边形边界的多房间结构，且缺乏对连续3D物理定律的深度理解，导致严重的几何穿透和漂浮伪影。

Method: 宏观层面：构建多楼层数据集微调自回归布局生成器，将文本解析为多边形边界和多楼层连通性的3D建筑基础。微观层面：引入Affordance-driven Physical-Semantic Scene Graph (A-PSSG)将物理可供性抽象为节点属性，建立严格的几何和语义边约束；将场景实例化建模为约束闭环优化问题，集成几何碰撞检测引擎与多模态大语言模型的高层语义推理，通过观察-评估-修改机制动态执行物理感知动作。

Result: Text2Villa在多项指标上优于以往方法，能够稳健地从文本生成高保真、物理合理的别墅级3D环境。

Conclusion: Text2Villa为下游应用提供了可靠且可交互的3D内容基础，在生成物理合理的多楼层室内场景方面取得了显著进展。

Abstract: Generating 3D indoor scenes from natural language holds tremendous potential, yet existing methods predominantly fail to generate multi-room structures with vertical connectivity and arbitrary polygonal boundaries. Furthermore, they lack a deep grounding in continuous 3D physical laws, leading to severe geometric penetrations and floating artifacts. In this work, we propose Text2Villa, a novel hierarchical generative framework. At the macro level, we construct a multi-story dataset to fine-tune an autoregressive layout generator, ensuring the direct parsing of text into 3D building foundations featuring polygonal boundaries and multi-story connectivity. To enforce physical laws during micro-level asset arrangement, we introduce the Affordance-driven Physical-Semantic Scene Graph (A-PSSG) to explicitly abstract physical affordances (such as support surfaces and containment cavities) into node attributes, establishing strict geometric and semantic edge constraints. Guided by the A-PSSG, we formulate scene instantiation as a constrained closed-loop optimization problem following the analysis-by-synthesis paradigm. By integrating an underlying geometric collision detection engine with the high-level semantic reasoning of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), our heuristic solver dynamically executes physics-aware actions under the observation-evaluation-modification mechanism to effectively resolve mesh collisions, floating artifacts, and fine-grained cavity containment failures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Text2Villa outperforms previous methods across various metrics, robustly generating high-fidelity and physically plausible villa-level 3D environments from text, thereby providing a reliable and interactive 3D content foundation for downstream applications.

</details>


### [279] [Feature-Guided Diffusion for Non-Differentiable Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17411)
*Andrei-Timotei Ardelean,Michael Fischer,Tim Weyrich,Tomáš Iser*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出特征引导扩散进化(FIDE)框架，利用ViT提取密集视觉特征训练扩散模型生成候选解，再通过CMA进化策略迭代优化，无需梯度或特定初始化即可解决逆渲染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可微渲染器需大量工程定制且易陷入局部极小值；无梯度方法虽减少工程需求但严重依赖良好初始化。现有方法均无法在完全黑箱条件下可靠地解决逆问题。

Method: 将渲染器视为黑箱函数；使用ViT从候选渲染中提取密集视觉特征替代标量损失；利用这些特征训练扩散模型作为候选提议网络；扩散模型提议的解与CMA进化策略形成闭环迭代优化。

Result: 在路径追踪、向量样条、Voronoi着色器和机器人等多个逆问题任务上验证，特征引导相比标量损失基线显著提升收敛性，能可靠逃离梯度方法陷入的局部极小值。

Conclusion: FIDE作为全黑箱框架无需梯度或特定初始化，通过特征引导有效解决逆渲染中的歧义性和局部极小值问题，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Inverse rendering is traditionally solved via differentiable renderers and gradient descent, which requires substantial problem-specific engineering and is prone to getting stuck in local minima due to ambiguities. Derivative-free approaches alleviate engineering requirements, but often heavily depend on a good problem initialization. In this work, we propose Feature-Informed Diffusion Evolution (FIDE), a fully black-box framework that requires no gradients or specific initialization: the renderer is treated as an opaque function whose only requirement is to produce images. Our key insight is feature guiding: rather than reducing each candidate rendering to a scalar loss value, we use a Vision Transformer (ViT) to extract dense visual features from it. We subsequently use these features to train a diffusion-based candidate proposal model, allowing the network to use visual cues to predict parameters that would match the target image. The candidate solutions proposed by this diffusion model are then refined in a closed loop with a CMA evolution strategy, continuously narrowing the proposal region as optimization progresses. We validate across diverse inverse problems from path tracing, vector splines, Voronoi shaders, and robotics, and demonstrate that feature-guiding substantially improves convergence over scalar-loss baselines and reliably escapes local minima where gradient-based methods stall.

</details>


### [280] [Fast VEM Fluid Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17725)
*Runze Zhang,Bo Ren*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: FastVEM 是一个高效的边界适形流体模拟框架，通过虚拟元方法（VEM）离散化、保凸切割网格策略和 Galerkin 几何多重网格求解器的协同设计，在复杂边界条件下将压力投影阶段的计算速度提升高达 100 倍，同时保持高保真度的流固耦合模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流固耦合产生的复杂运动在视觉上吸引人，但在复杂边界存在时模拟既困难又计算昂贵。传统的切割网格方法虽然能精确解析边界几何，但会导致压力投影阶段出现病态线性系统，带来难以承受的计算开销。

Method: 采用虚拟元方法（VEM）离散化以鲁棒地强制执行不可压缩条件和边界条件；使用 VEM 多项式空间中的粒子网格法（PIC）进行平流处理；提出保凸切割网格策略来构建适合模拟的体适形网格；开发 Galerkin 几何多重网格求解器，采用无扩散延拓算子防止粗层矩阵稠密化，并构建嵌套的边界感知网格层次以确保粗层自由度的适定放置。

Result: 与基于切割网格的传统流体模拟器相比，FastVEM 在计算上占主导地位的压力投影阶段实现了高达 100 倍的加速，同时能够鲁棒地处理更具挑战性的边界几何。

Conclusion: FastVEM 通过数值离散化、网格构建和线性求解器的自上而下协调设计，在保持高保真度的同时大幅降低了计算成本，为复杂边界条件下的流固耦合模拟提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The intricate motion arising from fluid--boundary interactions is visually compelling, yet notoriously difficult and computationally expensive to simulate in the presence of complex boundaries. Accurately resolving boundary geometry requires body-fitted grids constructed via cut-cell methods, which often leads to poorly conditioned linear systems in the pressure projection stage and, consequently, prohibitive computational cost. We present FastVEM, an efficient boundary-conforming fluid simulation framework that enables high-fidelity flow--boundary interaction at substantially reduced cost. Computational efficiency is achieved through a coordinated, top-down design spanning numerical discretization, grid construction, and linear solvers. FastVEM adopts a Virtual Element Method (VEM) discretization to robustly enforce incompressibility and boundary conditions on irregular body-fitted grids, and employs a VEM polynomial-space Particle-in-Cell scheme for advection. Complementing this discretization, a convexity-preserving cut-cell strategy is introduced to construct simulation-friendly body-fitted grids. To accelerate pressure projection, we develop a Galerkin geometric multigrid solver featuring a diffusion-free prolongation operator that prevents coarse-level matrix densification, along with a nested, boundary-aware grid hierarchy that ensures well-posed placement of coarse-level degrees of freedom. Compared to prior cut-cell--based fluid simulators, FastVEM speeds up the computationally dominant pressure projection stage by up to 100x, while robustly handling even more challenging boundary geometries.

</details>


### [281] [Packet-Loss Robust 3D Gaussian Compression via Atomic Packaging and GNN-based Error Concealment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17916)
*Yuxuan Tao,Xuerui Ma,Hao Zhang,Chunhua Peng*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向3D高斯溅射（3DGS）的鲁棒传输与错误隐藏框架。编码端通过锚点级原子封装和分层随机分组将数据包丢失转化为干净锚点缺失；解码端通过上下文感知残差插值（CARI）分支和轻量级图神经网络进行先验感知属性修复，在20%随机丢包下相比无隐藏传输显著提升，PSNR损失控制在约3 dB以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方案（如HAC++）的比特流在网络流传输中易因丢包产生属性不一致的损坏锚点，导致严重渲染伪影。现有压缩方法常将相关锚点属性分离到独立流中，单个数据包丢失即可引发属性不一致问题。

Method: 编码端：锚点级原子封装将锚点所有属性联合打包，将属性损坏转化为锚点缺失；分层随机分组在空间域分散丢包。解码端：提出先验感知属性修复框架，包含CARI分支（利用哈希网格先验预测与相邻残差构建鲁棒基线）和轻量级两层图神经网络（带跨注意力机制，修复高频属性残差）；属性级置信度控制在学习预测不可靠时回退至插值。

Result: 在BungeeNeRF、Mip-NeRF 360和Tanks and Temples数据集上，20%随机丢包条件下，所提方法相比无隐藏传输大幅提升，平均PSNR损失相对于无损HAC++参考控制在约3 dB以内。

Conclusion: 提出首个面向3DGS的丢包鲁棒传输与错误隐藏框架，通过编码端原子封装与解码端先验感知属性修复有效应对网络丢包，显著降低渲染伪影。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and recent compression schemes such as HAC++ enable high-fidelity real-time neural rendering, but their bitstreams are fragile under packet loss during network streaming. Existing compression methods often separate correlated anchor attributes into independent streams, so losing one packet can create attribute-inconsistent broken anchors and severe rendering artifacts. We propose a packet-loss robust 3DGS transmission and error concealment framework. On the encoder side, anchor-level atomic packaging jointly encapsulates all attributes of each anchor, converting corrupted-attribute failures into clean missing-anchor erasures. Stratified random grouping further disperses packet losses across the spatial domain to avoid large contiguous voids. On the decoder side, we formulate recovery as prior-aware attribute inpainting. A Context-Aware Residual Interpolation (CARI) branch uses hash-grid prior predictions and neighboring residuals to build a robust baseline, while a lightweight two-layer graph neural network with cross-attention over hash-grid priors refines high-frequency attribute residuals. Attribute-wise confidence control falls back to interpolation when learned predictions are unreliable. Experiments under 20 percent random packet loss on BungeeNeRF, Mip-NeRF 360, and Tanks and Temples show that the proposed method substantially improves over no-concealment transmission and limits average PSNR degradation to about 3 dB relative to the lossless HAC++ reference.

</details>


### [282] [EVOLVE: Efficient Learned Volume Compression with Variable-Rate Encoding on a Cross-Domain Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18187)
*Kaiyuan Tang,Maizhe Yang,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: EVOLVE是一个基于自编码器(AE)的科学数据体积压缩框架，通过大规模跨域数据库预训练、宏微观架构重新设计以及可学习的变速率增益机制，在保持高重建质量的同时实现远超传统压缩方法的压缩比，且压缩速度比隐式神经表示(INR)方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学模拟产生的体数据远超存储和网络带宽的承载能力，亟需高效的有损压缩。传统压缩器在高压缩比下难以保留精细结构细节，而隐式神经表示(INR)方法需要昂贵的逐体积优化且产生固定压缩比的模型，无法灵活适应不同需求。

Method: (1) 构建了包含来自21个科学模拟的6,376个体数据的大规模跨域数据库，通过感知哈希确保多样性；(2) 重新审视AE压缩器设计空间，在基础AE中融入宏微观设计以提升表达能力和压缩能力；(3) 开发可学习的增益机制配合三阶段训练策略，实现单模型支持推理时连续调节压缩比的可变速率编码。

Result: 在多个未见过的科学模拟数据集上，EVOLVE在相当的重建质量下实现了远超传统压缩器的压缩比，且压缩速度比INR方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: EVOLVE作为压缩科学数据的有力新方案，展示了基于自编码器的框架在高压缩比、高质量和快速压缩方面相较于传统方法和INR方法的显著优势。

Abstract: Large-scale scientific simulations generate volumetric data at rates that far outpace advances in storage and network bandwidth, making effective lossy compression increasingly critical. However, conventional compressors often struggle to preserve fine structural details at high compression ratios (CRs), and implicit neural representations (INRs) require costly per-volume optimization and produce models with fixed CRs. To respond, we present EVOLVE, an autoencoder (AE)-based volume-compression framework that targets high CRs for offline compression, with three key contributions. First, we construct a large-scale cross-domain database of 6,376 volumes from 21 scientific simulations, curated via perceptual hashing to ensure diversity, enabling the optimized model to extract features that generalize across volumes within the covered scientific simulation domains. Second, we reexamine the design space of AE-based compressors and incorporate several macro- and micro-designs into a vanilla AE to develop EVOLVE, which substantially improves the expressive power and compression capability. Third, we develop a learnable gain mechanism with a three-stage training strategy to enable variable-rate encoding, allowing a single model to support continuous CR adjustment at inference time. Experiments on multiple unseen scientific simulation datasets demonstrate that EVOLVE achieves substantially higher CRs than conventional compressors at comparable reconstruction quality, while delivering compression speeds that are orders of magnitude faster than INR-based methods, highlighting its promise as a strong alternative for compressing scientific data. The code, model weights, and results are available on our project page at https://evolve-vis.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [A Practical Guide to Simulating Correlated Binary Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16801)
*Chi Heem Wong,Zied Ben Chaouch*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文针对具有指定均值和Pearson相关系数的二元Bernoulli结果的模拟问题，指出高斯阈值方法在阈值化后无法正确匹配目标相关性，并提出了一种基于联合概率质量函数（PMF）的线性规划框架，能够精确匹配一阶和二阶矩或证明不可行性，同时给出了凸包表征和截断矩完成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元Bernoulli结果的模拟在风险管理中很常见，高斯阈值方法（将潜在高斯相关性设置为目标Bernoulli相关性）在阈值化后通常不正确，而tetrachoric校准仅在潜在矩阵半正定时才精确，因此需要一种直接基于PMF的精确可行性与模拟框架。

Method: 将问题转化为关于$2^N$个原子概率的线性规划，施加归一化、非负性、均值约束和成对交叉矩约束；通过凸包表征证明可行目标存在至多$1+N+\binom{N}{2}$个状态的支撑集，不可行目标存在二次分离证书；开发了截断矩完成方案（通过顺序条件生成样本）和稀疏支撑工作集精化方法以降低内存使用。

Result: 所提出的PMF公式要么返回精确匹配一阶和二阶矩的分布律，要么证明不可行性；凸包表征给出了支撑集大小的上界；截断矩完成方案可在中等维度下进行可行性与模拟分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为中等维度和结构化替代场景提供了精确的PMF基础，同时澄清了高斯阈值构造的局限性——即设置潜在高斯相关性等于目标Bernoulli相关性通常不正确。

Abstract: Simulating dependent Bernoulli outcomes with prescribed means and pairwise Pearson correlations is a common task in risk modeling. A familiar approach is the Gaussian-threshold workflow for binary outcomes, often viewed as a Bernoulli analogue of the Gaussian copula construction. We show that setting latent Gaussian correlations equal to target Bernoulli correlations is generally incorrect after thresholding, and that pairwise tetrachoric calibration is exact only when the calibrated latent matrix is positive semidefinite. We therefore formulate the problem directly over the joint Bernoulli probability mass function. Given target means and pairwise correlations, we impose normalization, nonnegativity, mean constraints, and pairwise cross-moment constraints as a linear program over the $2^N$ atomic probabilities. The resulting PMF formulation either returns an exact law matching the requested first and second moments or certifies infeasibility. A convex-hull characterization further shows that every feasible target admits a law supported on at most $1+N+\binom{N}{2}$ states, while every infeasible target admits a separating quadratic certificate. We then develop a truncated-moment completion scheme that fits a reduced cross-moment table and generates samples by sequential conditioning, together with a sparse-support working-set refinement that can reduce memory usage on structured instances, although the worst-case complexity remains exponential. Together, these constructions provide an exact PMF-based framework for feasibility and simulation at moderate dimension and structured alternatives when the full atomic representation is impractical, while clarifying the limits of Gaussian-threshold constructions.

</details>


### [284] [Efficient Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design for Expensive Computational Models over Finite Design Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16933)
*Maximilian Dinkel,Dragos C. Ana,Benedikt Goderbauer,Wolfgang A. Wall*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对有限设计集的自适应贝叶斯最优实验设计算法，通过加速嵌套蒙特卡洛估计、公共随机数和Rao-Blackwellization方差缩减技术，以及基于bootstrap采样的迭代淘汰策略，在显著降低计算成本的同时实现高可靠性，适用于大规模工程应用中的昂贵计算模型校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯校准中，当数据不足或不合适时，参数识别存在显著不确定性，影响决策和系统理解。而实验设计变量（如传感器位置、加载条件）通常只能从有限集合中选择，现有方法在处理昂贵计算模型时计算成本过高。

Method: 1. 加速嵌套蒙特卡洛估计器，通过重用参数样本减少模型评估次数；2. 采用公共随机数和Rao-Blackwellization降低成对期望信息增益比较的方差；3. 使用bootstrap抽样估计一个设计优于另一个的概率；4. 从少量样本开始，基于概率比较迭代淘汰劣质设计，将计算资源分配给有前景的候选设计，直至剩余单一设计。

Result: 该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时实现了高可靠性，适用于大规模工程应用。

Conclusion: 该自适应算法有效解决了有限设计集下昂贵计算模型的贝叶斯最优实验设计问题，通过智能资源分配和方差缩减技术，平衡了计算效率与决策可靠性。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration is a powerful framework for identifying parameters in complex and large-scale computational models. However, when there is insufficient or poorly suited data for the calibration process, significant uncertainty about the identified parameters remains. This uncertainty can hinder effective decision-making and understanding of the system. In many applications, the experiment that generates the data can be influenced by design variables, such as sensor placements, loading conditions, or test configurations. Often, however, these design variables can not be chosen arbitrarily but only from a finite set of possible experimental designs. We propose an adaptive algorithm for Bayesian optimal experimental design over a finite design set, specifically tailored for applications involving expensive computational models. The method integrates an accelerated nested Monte Carlo estimator that reuses parameter samples to reduce model evaluations. Additionally, it employs common random numbers and Rao--Blackwellization to reduce variance in pairwise expected information gain comparisons. To support decision-making within the algorithm, we estimate the probability that one design outperforms another using bootstrap sampling.
